# ROUTES INDEX — row D (Weil positivity on the declared carrier), phases 100–189

Scope: 03-research phases 100–189 (incl. SYNTHESIS_188, NT_SYNTHESIS, adversarial reviews), master grid R162_2300 (walls §3, falsifiers §4, tools §5, never-tried §6, log §8 entries 1–48), 05-meta-and-planning (CROSSWORD-TO-D, PATH-TO-D, ROW-D-REDUCED-STATEMENT, HANDOFF-149, PLAN, INVENTORY-168-184), Paper 47 barrier sections, Paper 46 §18/§27/§28.4 (T1–T5, polar/entire).
Label convention: the grid's old killer tag "E1" is rewritten here as **REF-D** = "the route ends in an internal reformulation of D (no closure, no impossibility)"; REF-D is NOT a closure criterion — such routes are listed as reformulations, not as killed. Row D status throughout: **OPEN; no certified negative value; no sign contact.**
Outcome vocabulary: DEAD (route closed in the record, killer named) / NO-GO (impossibility proved within a declared method class) / FALSIFIED (explicit counter-model) / PARTIAL (what survives) / OPEN-RESIDUE.
Part I = curated (walls, falsifiers, paper barriers, phases 185–189 + key 100–161 items). Part II = every grid route attributed to phases 100–184 (auto-extracted from R162_2300_MASTER_grid.json, deduplicated, killer tags sanitized).
